def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    import numpy as np, time, math
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed ^ 0x9e3779b9)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 2.0) # leave 2s
    def score_arr(a):
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline: return np.inf
        try:
            s = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
        except Exception:
            s = np.inf
        return float(s)
    candidates=[] # maybe not store all
    def consider(a):
       nonlocal best, best_score # if nested in Python use list
```
Python 3 nonlocal fine. But top-level function. Could avoid nested nonlocal by variables.

Initialize best:
```
best = None; best_score = np.inf
def add_candidate(a):
    nonlocal best,best_score
    if a is None: return
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0 or not np.isfinite(a).all(): return
    if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline: return
    s = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
        best = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0).copy()
        best_score = s
```
But `evaluate_sequence` does clip; for rounding search, do we want pass exactly candidate array; `tolist` of clipped? evaluate clips again. If candidate has negative tiny from perturbation, clip changes. Use nonnegative ensure.

Let's design objective for hill climbing:
```
def conv_max(a, fft_cache, q=8):
    # a float length n sum maybe 1
    F = rfft(a, N)
    c = irfft(F*F, N)[:L]
    if any negative: c = np.maximum(c,0)
    M = c.max()
    if M <=0: return 0, c
    # surrogate to smooth: (np.mean(c**q))**1/q maybe; plus final M? Use soft_max:
    # obj = M + (cmax-c_min? no)
    # For hill climb, use E = np.sum(c^q) (scale not matter)
    E = np.sum(c**q)
    return M, E
```

Let's decipher the LP approach:
"Starting from a nonnegative step function f normalized sum = sqrt(2n), set M=||f*f||∞. Next compute g0 by solving an LP maximizing sum b subject to b≥0 and ||f*g0||_∞≤M; as is standard, optimum is attained at an extreme point determined by active set of binding inequalities, here corresponding to important constraints where the convolution bound (f*g0)(x)≤M is tight and limiting. Rescale g0 to normalization, update f←(1-t)f+tg. Repeating produces sequence with nonincreasing ||f*f||∞." This sounds like a method for minimizing `||f*f||∞` under `||f*g||∞` constraints? They fix M from f and find another feasible g with same "cross-convolution" bound, maximizing sum. It might be related to_density? It assumes if f*g≤M and g*g? But maybe through positive semidefinite? Actually if f*g ≤ M for all shifts, and f normalized, it defines a candidate g with same "autocorrelation" bound? Wait the autocorrelation upper bound AC1 maybe uses two functions f and g? The original problem maybe maximizing something for a filter with side-lobes? They optimize step heights of a correlator? Not sure. But evaluator uses f*f max. LP on f*g may produce g with lower ||g*g||∞ due duality? Let's derive.

If we want find g minimizing ||g*g||∞ under sum fixed. KKT conditions for min max: For fixed t, at optimum there exist multipliers λ_k≥0 for constraints g*g≤t, active. Gradient: 0 = λ-weighted B_k g - ν (for sum) - μ (nonneg). This is an eigenvalue-like. Their LP has constraints f*g≤M, not g*g. It's perhaps a Frank-Wolfe type: current f, linearize max g*g at f: max_k (f*g)_k ≤ M (since at f, g=f gives f*f=M). Then maximize sum g subject to linearized constraints. That is FRWK: minimize convex-looking? At point f, T(g)=max g*g nonconvex, but gradient/linearization uses f*g? For quadratic T, tangent at f is max_k dual? Since T(g) ≈ f*g on active? The tangent plane to max_k B_k? For nonconvex max of quadratics, use linearization c_k(g) ≈ c_k(f)+∇, but if we restrict scale, maybe just cross term? They maximize sum g subject to f*g≤M, which is exactly the first-order cones of each quadratic: c_k(f+δ)=c_k(f)+2 f*δ+δ*δ. For small δ, c_k≤M approx f*δ≤0? If M=f*f. Maximizing sum with f*g≤M might move in tangent feasible directions. As δ small, f*g approximately M + ∇c_active δ, so constraints active give gradient projection. It is a Frank-Wolfe step for nonconvex max of quadratics. This can be efficient and is SOTA.

Can we implement the LP numerically? For n=2535, maybe yes using scipy.optimize.linprog? Let's evaluate. Variables b length n. Constraints f*b ≤ M for each k (2n-1 constraints), b≥0. Maximize sum b. def convolve_fft(a, L, N):
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:L]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0? actually negative tiny: c = np.maximum(c,0)
    return c

def select_constraints(c, M, max_constraints):
    Lc = len(c)
    thresh = M - (0.25*M + 1e-12)  # top 25% maybe
    idx = np.nonzero(c >= thresh)[0]

    # ensure include center? if not, add n-1
    return sorted(idx.tolist())

Convolution via FFT:
```
def make_fft_plan(n):
    L=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N<L: N <<=1
    return L,N

def conv(a, L, N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:L]
    # small negative due roundoff
    # do not zero all negatives? For max, negatives irrelevant. Use c = c if c>0 else 0 for M.
    return c

def max_conv(a):
   c=conv(a)
   return np.max(c)
```
For scoring, if sum not 1: score=2n max/(sum^2). For optimization, we normalize to sum1 after each update; so M is objective normalized.

But if c has slight negative? np.max nonnegative.

LP constraint selection and matrix:
```
def constraints_matrix(ks,n):
   m=len(ks)
   indices=[]; indptr=[0]
   for k in ks:
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      # hi inclusive, length hi-lo+1
      indices.extend(range(lo,hi+1))
      indptr.append(len(indices))
   data=np.ones(len(indices), dtype=np.float64)
   A=csr_matrix((data, np.asarray(indices,dtype=np.int32), indptr), shape=(m,n))
   return A
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    import numpy as np, time, scipy.sparse as sp, scipy.optimize as opt
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0,budget_s-30)
    # choose n
    n=2535
    tries = []
    def exact_score(arr): return evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.clip(arr,0,1000)])
    def update_best(arr):...
    # create initial candidates
    t_grid = np.arange(n)/n
    # uniform
    a0 = np.full(n,1.0); a0/=a0.sum()
    # sine/exponential
    for rho in (0.3,0.6,0.9,1.0):
       for phase in (0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5):
          p = np.sin(2*np.pi*t_grid + phase)
          base = 1.0 + rho*p
          # if min <=0, use exp
          if np.min(base) <=0: base = np.exp(rho*p)
          a=base.clip(min=1e-6); a/=a.sum(); update
          b=np.exp(rho*p); b/=b.sum(); update
    # random smooth 3 modes
    for _ in range(10):
       p = sum amp_k*sin(...)+...
       a=np.exp(0.5*p).clip?
    # keep top 3 starts by exact score
```

```
def fw_refine(a, max_time):
    a=a.copy(); a=np.clip(a,1e-12,None); a/=a.sum()
    L,N=...; M=max(conv(a))
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>deadline: break
       c=conv(a); M=c.max()
       # select constraints based on c: top threshold
       order = np.argsort(c)[::-1] maybe 5069 sort cheap.
       # choose k until c[idx] < R
       th = M - max(0.05*M, 1e-8?)? Need active set small.
       ks=[]; for idx in order: if len(ks)>=maxA or c[idx] < M - active_decay: break; ks.append(idx)
       # if ks too small (<10), add top 10.
       if M <= 0: break
       # Maybe use all constraints if M close to target? Active count can increase.
       maxA = min(L, 200 + it*20?)? grow.
       Build A.
       c_obj = -np.ones(n)
       try:
          res = opt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(len(ks), M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit':25, 'presolve':True})
       except Exception: res=None
       if res.x None: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,float).clip(0,None)
       s=g.sum()
       if s<=0: break
       g/=s
       # line search t values
       ts=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]
       best_t=0; best_M=M
       for t in ts:
          cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cand/=cand.sum()
          Mc = max_conv_fft(cand)
          if Mc < best_M - 1e-12: best...
       if best_t>0:
          a=(1-best_t)*old + ...
          M=best_M
          # exact evaluate and update best exact
          exact=exact_score(a); update_best
          no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          # perturb a to escape? maybe add small random smooth
          a = normalize(clip(a * (1+0.02*rng.normal...)?,1e-12)) # maybe
          # if no improve many, break
       if it>... and no_improve>5: maybe try smaller active count/random; break
    return a,M
```

def random_smooth(n, rng, K=8, amp=0.6):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    p=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       ampl=rng.uniform(0.1, amp/k**0.5)
       # choose cos/sin or phase
       p += ampl * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + ph)
    a=np.exp(0.7*p)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

def fw_refine(a, until, max_iter=60):
    a=normalize(a)
    M=max_conv_fft(a)
    # exact evaluate a at start
    update_best(a)
    stall=0
    active_limit=80
    it=0
    while it<max_iter and time.monotonic() < until:
        c=conv(a)
        M=np.max(c)
        # if M not finite break
        order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        # threshold and limits
        target = min(max_active, max(min_active, int((2*n-1)*0.15)))? maybe
        # active all c near M within decay.
        decay = max(0.08*M, 1e-10)
        ks_list=[]
        for idx in order:
           if c[idx] < M - decay: break
           ks_list.append(idx)
        if len(ks_list) < 30:
           ks_list = order[:30].tolist()
        if len(ks_list) > max_active:
           ks_list = order[:max_active].tolist()
        elif len(ks_list) < max_active and max_active < 1000:
           # add some next top to broaden? Maybe not necessary
           pass
        max_active += int(max(20, max_active*0.5)) # grow slowly
        # Build LP matrix
        A = build_matrix(ks_list,n)
        ub = max(20.0/n, 0.001)
        bounds = [(0.0, ub)]*n
        try:
            res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(len(ks_list),M), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit':20, 'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
        except Exception:
            res=None
        if not res or res.x is None: break
        g=np.asarray(res.x).clip(0,None)
        sg=g.sum()
        if sg <=1e-12: continue
        g/=sg
        # include g maybe normalized; if g too close to a skip? 
        ts = [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]
        best_cand=None; best_M=M
        for t in ts:
           cand=a + t*(g-a)
           if cand.min()<0: cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
           cand/=cand.sum()
           Mc=np.max(conv(cand))
           if Mc < best_M - 1e-12:
              best_M=Mc; best_cand=cand; best_t=t
        if best_cand is not None:
           a=best_cand
           update_best(a)
           stall=0
        else:
           # Try smaller t? maybe include 0.01..
           # If no improvement, active limit increase, add random perturbation
           # Perturb a with small smooth noise
           p = rng.normal(size=n)*0.01? Could disrupt. Use block multiplicative.
           warp = 1.0 + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
           # smooth warp via FFT? Could just random small
           perturb = a * np.exp(0.05*rng.standard_normal(n))
           perturb = normalize(perturb)
           # evaluate; if improves accept, else keep a
           if np.max(conv(perturb)) < M - 1e-12:
              a=perturb; update_best(a)
           else:
              # if no improvement, maybe accept small t even if surrogate no? no.
           stall +=1
           if stall > 5: break
        it +=1
    return a
```

Function choose `ks`:
```
def choose_ks(c, M, max_ks, min_ks):
    L=len(c)
    # sort slice top max_ks maybe use np.argpartition for speed
    if L > max_ks:
       # top max_ks by c using argpartition, then sort by c desc within
       part = np.argpartition(c, -max_ks)[-max_ks:]
       top = part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
    else:
       top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    # Among top, select those with c >= M - decay, but at least min_ks.
    decay = max(0.02*M, 1e-12)
    # since top sorted desc
    sel=[]
    for k in top:
        if c[k] < M - decay:
            break
        sel.append(k)
        if len(sel) >= max_ks: break
    if len(sel) < min_ks:
       # fill from top to min_ks (or max_ks)
       need = min(max_ks, max(min_ks, len(sel)))
       for k in top[len(sel):]:
          if len(sel) >= need: break
          sel.append(k)
    # ensure include center n-1? If not, insert
    if (n-1) not in sel:
       sel.append(n-1)
    return sorted(sel)
```

def random_local(a, until):
  a=normalize(a); M=max_conv(a); update_best
  stall=0
  while time<until:
    cand=a.copy()
    # choose type
    if rng.random()<0.5: # block
      width=rng.integers(2, max(3,n//20))
      start=rng.integers(0,n-width)
      cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.15))
    else: # two-point mass move
      i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n)); if i==j continue
      d = 0.05*a.mean() * rng.normal(0,1) # small
      # move mass: ensure nonnegative
      amt = min(a[i], 0.05) * rng.uniform(0.1,1.0) * (1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1)
      if a[i] + amt>0 and a[j]-amt>0 ...
    cand=normalize(cand)
    Mc=max_conv(cand)
    if Mc < M - 1e-12:
       a=cand; M=Mc; update_best; stall=0
    else:
       stall+=1
       if stall>1000: break?
```

```
def build_matrix(ks,n):
    m=len(ks); indptr=[0]
    parts=[]
    for k in ks:
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       parts.append(np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=np.int32))
    indptr = np.cumsum([0]+[p.size for p in parts], dtype=np.int32) # but cumsum int? fine
    indices=np.concatenate(parts) if parts else empty
    data=np.ones(indices.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return sp.csr_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(m,n))
```

def select_ks(c, M, max_ks, min_ks):
   L=len(c)
   if max_ks >= L:
      top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
   else:
      top = np.argpartition(c, -max_ks)[-max_ks:]
      top = top[np.argsort(c[top])[::-1]]
   decay=max(0.02*M, 1e-12)
   sel=[]
   for k in top:
       if c[k] < M - decay: break
       sel.append(int(k))
       if len(sel) >= max_ks: break
   if len(sel) < min_ks:
       # fill from top starting after existing (top sorted)
       # create set maybe
       for k in top:
          if len(sel) >= min( max_ks, max(min_ks, len(sel)) )? Actually target_len = min(max_ks, max(min_ks, len(sel)))

          if k not in sel_set: sel.append
       # simpler: just take first min_ks from top if threshold not enough
       if len(sel) < min_ks:
           sel = [int(k) for k in top[:min_ks]]
       elif len(sel) < max_ks:
           # maybe extend to threshold already no; do nothing
           pass
   if len(sel) > max_ks:
       sel = sel[:max_ks]
   # ensure center
   c_idx = n-1
   if c_idx not in sel:
       # if len<max_ks append else replace lowest?
       if len(sel) < max_ks: sel.append(c_idx)
       else: sel[-1]=c_idx
   return sorted(set(sel))
```

def grad_refine(a, until, q=8, max_iter=300):
    a=normalize(a); M=max_conv(a); update_best(a)
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time>until: break
        c=conv(a); M=c.max()
        # if M <=0 break
        w=(c/M)**(q-1)  # if M=0
        # zero out tiny negatives? c max positive, c may have tiny neg; use np.maximum(c,0)
        w=np.where(c>0, (c/max(c,1e-300))**(q-1),0)
        g = 2*crossgrad(a,w)
        m=np.dot(a,g)
        delta=g-m
        var=delta.std()
        if not finite or var<1e-15:
            break
        # Try eta values scaled by 1/(var+1) maybe

             eta_scaled = eta * scale? Hmm.
```

Potential bug in `crossgrad` formula with FFT: Need use `tcrossN` maybe. We'll implement general:
```
def crossgrad(a,w):
    # a length n, w length L=2n-1
    N = 1 << (a.size + w.size -1).bit_length()
    ar = a[::-1]
    F = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    G = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    tmp = np.fft.irfft(F*G, N)
    return tmp[n-1:n-1+n]
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):

def conv(a):

def cross_grad(a,w):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Ncross)
    G = np.fft.rfft(w, Ncross)
    tmp = np.fft.irfft(F*G, Ncross)
    return 2.0*tmp[n-1:n-1+n]
```

```
def refresh_best(arr, score=None):
   nonlocal best_arr, best_score
   arr = np.asarray(arr, float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return np.inf
   arr = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   if arr.sum() < 0.01: return np.inf
   if score is None:
       try: score = float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
       except Exception: return np.inf
   if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
       best_score=float(score); best_arr=arr.copy()
   return float(score) if np.isfinite(score) else np.inf
```

```
def fw_refine(a0, until):
    a=normalize(a0)
    # maybe exact initial
    refresh_best(a)
    it=0
    stall=0
    c=max(0,1)
    while it < 80 and time.monotonic() < until:
        now=time.monotonic()
        if until - now < 5: break
        c=max_conv? Actually need c for selection. Use c=conv(a); M=float(c.max())
        if not finite M or M<=0: break
        dec = max(0.03*M, 1e-12)
        max_ks = min(L, 60 + 50*it)  # up to? 
        if max_ks > 1200: max_ks=1200
        min_ks = min(max_ks, 30 + 5*it)
        # top
        if max_ks >= L:
            top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -max_ks)[-max_ks:]
            top = top[np.argsort(c[top])[::-1]]
        thres=[]
        for kk in top:
            if len(thres) >= max_ks: break
            if c[kk] < M - dec: break
            thres.append(int(kk))
        target_len = min(max_ks, max(min_ks, len(thres)))
        ks = [int(kk) for kk in top[:target_len]]
        if (n-1) not in ks:
            if len(ks) < max_ks: ks.append(n-1)
            else: ks[-1]=n-1
        ks=sorted(set(ks))
        # build A
        try:
           invM=1.0/M
           data_parts=[]; col_parts=[]; lengths=[]
           for kk in ks:
               lo=max(0, kk-n+1); hi=min(n-1, kk)
               if hi < lo: continue
               idx=np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=np.int32)
               col_parts.append(idx)
               data_parts.append((a[kk - idx] * invM).astype(np.float64))
               lengths.append(idx.size)
           if not lengths: break
           indptr=np.empty(len(ks)+1, dtype=np.int32) # if skipped? need m actual
        except: break
```
If skip rows due hi<lo (should not for valid ks 0..L-1), need actual m. Better build lists actual `ks_actual` and lengths. Since all ks valid, no skip. Use `m=len(ks)`. For each kk, idx size >0. Good.
```
           indptr = np.empty(m+1, dtype=np.int32); indptr[0]=0
           total=0
           for i,l in enumerate(lengths): total += l; indptr[i+1]=total
           indices=np.concatenate(col_parts); data=np.concatenate(data_parts)
           A=sp.csr_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(m,n))
           invM=1/M; ub = max(1.5*float(np.max(a)), 50.0/n, 1e-8)
           bounds=sp.optimize.Bounds(np.zeros(n), np.full(n, ub))
           b_ub=np.ones(m)
           c_obj=-np.ones(n)
           rem=max(2.0, (until - time.monotonic())*0.4)
           lp_time=max(2.0, min(30.0, rem))
           res=None
           for opts in [{'time_limit': lp_time}, {}]:
              try:
                 res=so.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
                 break
              except Exception:
                 continue
           if res is None or res.x is None: break
           g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
           if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): continue
           g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
           sg=g.sum()
           if sg <=1e-12: continue
           g/=sg
           directions=[]
           directions.append(g)
           mixed=0.75*g+0.25*a
           sm=mixed.sum()
           if sm>0: directions.append(mixed/sm)
           bestM=M; bestCand=None
           for direc in directions:
              for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
                 cand = a + t*(direc - a)
                 if cand.min()<0: cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
                 cs=cand.sum()
                 if cs<=0: continue
                 cand/=cs
                 Mc=float(np.max(conv(cand)))
                 if Mc < bestM - 1e-12:
                     bestM=Mc; bestCand=cand.copy(); bestDirec=direc; bestT=t
           if bestCand is not None:
               a=bestCand; stall=0; it+=1
               refresh_best(a)
               # if best_score < target by enough? do not break
           else:
               it +=1
               stall +=1
               # if stall, maybe try uniform small noise
               if stall >= 3 and rng.random()<0.5:
                   warp=1+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
                   cand=a*np.exp(0.05*rng.standard_normal(n))
                   cand=normalize(cand)
                   Mc=max_conv(cand)
                   if Mc < M -1e-12:
                      a=cand; refresh_best(a); stall=1; M=Mc
                   else:
                      M=bestM
                      if stall>6: break
    return a
```

def grad_refine(a0, until, max_iter=2000):

  M=max_conv(a); refresh_best(a)
  stall=0

    if M<=0: break

    g=cross_grad(a,w)
    if not finite: break

    delta=g-m
    sd=float(np.std(delta))
    if sd < 1e-15: break

def local_refine(a0, until):
   a=normalize(a0); M=max_conv(a); refresh_best(a)
   stall=0
   while time<until:
      if until-now<1: break
      cand=a.copy()
      r=rng.random()
      if r<0.6:
         width=int(rng.integers(2, max(3, min(64, n//20))))
         start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width))
         sigma = 0.2 if stall<50 else 0.07
         cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma))
      elif r<0.8:
         phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); amp=0.1
         cand *= np.exp(amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*t_grid+phase))
      else:
         i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
         if i!=j:
            amt = min(a[i], 0.02*a.sum()) * rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)
            if rng.random()<0.5:
               if a[i] >= amt: cand[i]-=amt; cand[j]+=amt
            else:
               if a[j] >= amt: cand[j]-=amt; cand[i]+=amt
      cand=normalize(cand)
      Mc=max_conv(cand)
      if Mc < M - 1e-12:
         a=cand; M=Mc; refresh_best(a); stall=0
      else:
         stall +=1
         if stall>500: break
   return a
```

   # For each original index interval x[j] to x[j+1], mass p[j] organic. Interpolate cumulative F(x).
   F=np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(p)])
   # nodes x extend 0, at each index center? For step function, CDF at points? Let's create x_nodes=np.linspace(0,1,m+1); F_nodes? But p_i corresponds interval [i/m,(i+1)/m]. mass at point i? For convolution, order matters; simple use `np.interp(y, x_centers, cumulative?)` not trivial.
       return np.interp(np.arange(n)/n, x, a) maybe if smooth.
```
Too complex. Skip resizing; if length mismatch, fallback uniform. Parent length 2535, we okay. For `refresh_best`, no resize.

Define normalize as:
```
if a.size != length:
    if a.size == 1: return np.full(length,1.0/length)
    return np.full(length, 1.0/length)
```
This may discard parent if length mismatch and we refine; but we only refine starts length n. If best_arr from parent length n, ok. If best_arr fallback [1], no grad.

```
indices = np.concatenate(col_parts) if col_parts else np.empty(0,dtype=np.int32)
data = np.concatenate(data_parts) if data_parts else np.empty(0)
del col_parts, data_parts
A = sp.csr_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(m,n))
del data, indices
```
This frees parts before A (A copies). Good.

One more potential mathematical check: In LP row weights scaled by `invM`: row dot g = (a*g)_k / M ≤1. Feasible a: (a*a)_k/M ≤1. Good. Objective max sum g (not scaled). If M changes, variables scale. Good.

The LP step uses selected constraints. If active set limited, a may violate unselected constraints for candidate; line search handles by evaluating max convolution. Since candidate(0) a feasible, for t small candidate likely feasible; t line search chooses if M decreases. Good.

Now, if `A` rows have all zero coefficients? Not possible for valid k and a positive? If a_i zeros, some rows may have all zeros if all a in invalid? Could happen for active peak? M nonzero, row c_k nonzero, so at least one product positive, so some a positive in row. Scaled row nonzero. Fine.

Now, in `fw_refine`, after accept, `M=bestM` but next loop recomputes. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy import sparse as sp
    from scipy import optimize as so
    SCIPY_OK = True
except Exception:
    sp = None
    so = None
    SCIPY_OK = False


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    # Leave a safety margin for the hard sandbox timeout.
    margin = 25.0 if budget_s > 120.0 else max(1.0, 0.05 * budget_s)
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - margin)
    if hard_deadline < t0 + 1.0:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 0.25)

    # Fixed working length.  This is large enough to approximate the
    # continuum problem, while dense convolutions of length ~ 2n remain cheap.
    n = 2535
    L = 2 * n - 1
    Nconv = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())
    Ncross = 1 << max(1, (n + L - 2).bit_length())

    best_arr = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def normalize(a, length=n):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != length:
            if a.size == 1:
                return np.full(length, 1.0 / length, dtype=np.float64)
            return np.full(length, 1.0 / length, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(length, 1.0 / length, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a / s
        return a

    def refresh_best(arr, score=None):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_score
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.inf
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(a.sum()) < 0.01:
            return np.inf
        if score is None:
            try:
                score = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                score = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_arr = a.copy()
        return float(score) if np.isfinite(score) else np.inf

    def conv(a):
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nconv)
        return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Nconv)[:L]

    def max_conv(a):
        return float(np.max(conv(a)))

    def cross_grad(a, w):
        # Gradient component for quadratic convolution objectives:
        # 2 * sum_j a_j w_{p+j}, p=0..n-1.
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Ncross)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, Ncross)
        tmp = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, Ncross)
        return 2.0 * tmp[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    # Basic valid fallbacks.
    refresh_best(np.ones(1))
    refresh_best(np.full(n, 1.0))

    # Use the supplied parent construction if present.
    try:
        parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent is not None:
            p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                refresh_best(p)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Start candidates: smooth, asymmetric nonnegative sequences.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    t_grid = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    starts = []

    def add_start(a):
        a = normalize(a, n)
        s = refresh_best(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            starts.append((float(s), a.copy()))

    start_until = min(t0 + 120.0, hard_deadline - 200.0)
    if time.monotonic() < start_until:
        add_start(np.full(n, 1.0))

        # Exponential sine one-parameter family and clipped sine family.
        for rho in (0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.3):
            for phi in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5):
                if time.monotonic() > start_until:
                    break
                z = 2.0 * np.pi * t_grid + phi
                add_start(np.exp(rho * np.sin(z)))
                base = 1.0 + rho * np.sin(z)
                if float(base.min()) > 0.0:
                    add_start(base)
            if time.monotonic() > start_until:
                break

        # Random low-order Fourier/Gaussian starts.
        attempts = 12
        for _ in range(attempts):
            if time.monotonic() > start_until:
                break
            if rng.random() < 0.6:
                z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                for k in range(1, 9):
                    amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.9) / (0.5 + 0.35 * float(k))
                    ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                    z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t_grid + ph)
                add_start(np.exp(0.45 * z))
            else:
                a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                for __ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 4))):
                    c = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)
                    w = rng.uniform(0.04, 0.28)
                    amp = rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0)
                    x = (t_grid - c) / w
                    a += amp * np.exp(-0.5 * x * x)
                add_start(a)

    if not starts:
        add_start(np.full(n, 1.0))

    starts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Frank-Wolfe-like Frank's LP direction:
    #   max sum(g)
    #   s.t. (a*g)_k <= M for a selected active set of k,
    #        0 <= g_i <= ub.
    # The rows are weighted by a_i / M.  This is the discrete analogue
    # of the parent construction's active-set LP step.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def fw_refine(a0, until):
        if not SCIPY_OK or so is None or sp is None:
            return normalize(a0, n)

        a = normalize(a0, n)
        refresh_best(a)
        it = 0
        stall = 0

        while it < 90 and time.monotonic() < until:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if until - now < 6.0:
                break

            c = conv(a)
            M = float(np.max(c))
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-15:
                break

            dec = max(3.0e-2 * M, 1e-12)

            cap = 1500
            max_ks = min(L, 60 + 50 * it, cap)
            min_ks = min(max_ks, 30 + 4 * it)

            if max_ks >= L:
                top = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
            else:
                top = np.argpartition(c, -max_ks)[-max_ks:]
                top = top[np.argsort(c[top])[::-1]]

            thres = []
            for kk in top:
                if len(thres) >= max_ks:
                    break
                if c[kk] < M - dec:
                    break
                thres.append(int(kk))

            target_len = min(max_ks, max(min_ks, len(thres)))
            ks = [int(kk) for kk in top[:target_len]]

            center = n - 1
            if center not in ks:
                if len(ks) < max_ks:
                    ks.append(center)
                else:
                    ks[-1] = center
            ks = sorted(set(ks))

            try:
                invM = 1.0 / M
                col_parts = []
                data_parts = []
                lengths = []

                for kk in ks:
                    lo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
                    hi = min(n - 1, kk)
                    if hi < lo:
                        continue
                    idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int32)
                    col_parts.append(idx)
                    # Row is (a * g)_k / M: coefficient of g_j is a_{k-j}/M.
                    data_parts.append((a[kk - idx] * invM).astype(np.float64))
                    lengths.append(int(idx.size))

                if not lengths:
                    break

                m = len(lengths)
                indptr = np.empty(m + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                indptr[0] = 0
                np.cumsum(lengths, dtype=np.int64, out=indptr[1:])

                indices = np.concatenate(col_parts) if col_parts else np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32)
                data = np.concatenate(data_parts) if data_parts else np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
                del col_parts, data_parts

                A = sp.csr_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(m, n))
                del data, indices

                ub = max(1.5 * float(np.max(a)), 50.0 / float(n), 1e-8)
                try:
                    bounds = so.Bounds(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), np.full(n, ub, dtype=np.float64))
                except Exception:
                    bounds = [(0.0, ub)] * n

                c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                # Tiny randomized tie-breaker so degenerate LP vertices can move.
                c_obj = c_obj - 1e-7 * rng.standard_normal(n)

                rem = max(2.0, float((until - time.monotonic()) * 0.45))
                lp_time = max(2.0, min(25.0, rem))

                res = None
                for opts in ({"time_limit": lp_time}, {}):
                    try:
                        res = so.linprog(
                            c_obj,
                            A_ub=A,
                            b_ub=np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64),
                            bounds=bounds,
                            method="highs",
                            options=opts,
                        )
                        break
                    except Exception:
                        continue

                if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
                    break

                g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                    continue
                g = np.max(g, 0.0) if g.ndim != 1 else g
                g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
                sg = float(g.sum())
                if sg <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                g = g / sg

                dirs = [g]
                mixed = 0.75 * g + 0.25 * a
                sm = float(mixed.sum())
                if sm > 1e-12:
                    dirs.append(mixed / sm)

                bestM = M
                best_cand = None

                for d in dirs:
                    for tstep in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
                        cand = a + tstep * (d - a)
                        if cand.min() < 0.0:
                            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                        cs = float(cand.sum())
                        if cs <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        cand = cand / cs
                        Mc = max_conv(cand)
                        if Mc < bestM - 1e-12:
                            bestM = Mc
                            best_cand = cand.copy()

                it += 1
                if best_cand is not None:
                    a = best_cand
                    stall = 0
                    refresh_best(a)
                else:
                    stall += 1
                    # Occasionally perturb to leave a bad LP vertex.
                    if stall >= 2 and rng.random() < 0.7:
                        cand = a * np.exp(0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                        cand = normalize(cand, n)
                        Mc = max_conv(cand)
                        if Mc < bestM - 1e-12:
                            a = cand
                            stall = 1
                            refresh_best(a)
                    if stall > 7:
                        break

            except Exception:
                break

        return a

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Full-sequence multiplicative subgradient polishing based on an
    # L^p energy with large p.  This uses only O(n log n) FFTs.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def grad_refine(a0, until, max_iter=2500):
        a = normalize(a0, n)
        refresh_best(a)
        stall = 0
        q = 8

        for it in range(max_iter):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= until:
                break
            if (now - t0) % 2 == 1 and until - now < 2.0:
                break

            c = conv(a)
            cpos = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            M = float(cpos.max())
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-15:
                break

            w = np.power(np.clip(cpos / M, 0.0, 1.0), q - 1)
            g = cross_grad(a, w)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                break

            gmean = float(np.dot(a, g))
            delta = g - gmean
            scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(delta * delta)))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
                break
            nd = np.clip(delta / scale, -5.0, 5.0)

            bestM = M
            best_cand = None
            for eta in (0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.045):
                cand = a * np.exp(-eta * nd)
                cand = normalize(cand, n)
                Mc = max_conv(cand)
                if Mc < bestM - 1e-12:
                    bestM = Mc
                    best_cand = cand

            if best_cand is not None:
                a = best_cand
                stall = 0
                refresh_best(a)
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 30:
                    break
                if stall > 6:
                    cand = normalize(a * np.exp(0.015 * rng.standard_normal(n)), n)
                    Mc = max_conv(cand)
                    if Mc < bestM - 1e-12:
                        a = cand
                        refresh_best(a)
                        stall = 2
                        M = Mc
                    else:
                        M = bestM

        return a

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Random sum-preserving local refinement, used as a final polish.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def local_refine(a0, until):
        a = normalize(a0, n)
        refresh_best(a)
        M = max_conv(a)
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() < until:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if until - now < 1.0:
                break

            cand = a.copy()
            r = rng.random()

            if r < 0.62:
                width = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, min(80, n // 20))))
                start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                sigma = 0.22 if stall < 60 else 0.08
                cand[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
            elif r < 0.82:
                phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                amp = 0.08 if stall < 60 else 0.03
                cand *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t_grid + phase))
            else:
                i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if i != j:
                    amt = min(float(a[i]), 0.02) * rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0)
                    if rng.random() < 0.5:
                        if a[i] >= amt:
                            cand[i] -= amt
                            cand[j] += amt
                    else:
                        if a[j] >= amt:
                            cand[j] -= amt
                            cand[i] += amt

            cand = normalize(cand, n)
            Mc = max_conv(cand)
            if Mc < M - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                M = Mc
                stall = 0
                refresh_best(a)
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 700:
                    break

        return a

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stage budget.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120.0:
        lp_until = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, hard_deadline - 170.0)
        if len(starts) > 0 and time.monotonic() < lp_until - 10.0:
            fw_refine(starts[0][1].copy(), lp_until)

        if len(starts) > 1 and starts[1][0] <= starts[0][0] + 0.10:
            lp2_until = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, hard_deadline - 160.0)
            if time.monotonic() < lp2_until - 10.0:
                fw_refine(starts[1][1].copy(), lp2_until)

        grad_until = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, hard_deadline - 60.0)
        if best_arr is not None and np.asarray(best_arr).size == n:
            grad_refine(best_arr.copy(), grad_until)

    local_until = max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, hard_deadline - 12.0)
    if best_arr is not None and np.asarray(best_arr).size == n:
        local_refine(best_arr.copy(), local_until)

    # Final exact validation before return.
    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        refresh_best(best_arr)
    else:
        refresh_best(best_arr)

    out = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if out.size == 0 or float(out.sum()) < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
